Thailand invites visitors to witness the Royal Barge Procession 2026 and rehearsals on Bangkok’s Chao Phraya River

Thailand invites visitors and the public to witness the Royal Barge Procession 2026 and 12 rehearsals along Bangkok’s Chao Phraya River. From 17 August to 6 November, these occasions offer rare opportunities to observe a centuries-old royal tradition culminating in the Royal Kathin Ceremony at Wat Arun Ratchawararam in Bangkok.
